- **pericles-org-memory**: How to build Organizational Memory — the customer's persistent context (documents, strategic customers/product lines, history) behind MCP, plus the §9 per-tenant cross-customer signal. - **pericles-plans-ui**: How to build Plans — the response-workflow module: a Plan Builder (form + drag-drop workflow graph), execution, a communications hub, reporting, and simulation. - **pericles-postgres-queue**: How to do queueing and key-value storage on PostgreSQL in Pericles — the deliberate replacement for Kafka and Redis. - **pericles-security-threat-model**: The Pericles threat model in build terms — what an attacker (or a poisoned data feed, or a malicious Custom Skill) could try, and the controls that stop it. Use this WHENEVER you handle untrusted content, add egress, build Custom Skill support, or review a security-sensitive change. Encodes the five tenant-isolation guarantees, prompt-injection defenses, the egress allowlist (MCP-only), PII/exfiltration prevention, the Custom Skill sandbox, and the P0 response. ([Pericles-Cloud/pericles](https://github.com/Pericles-Cloud/pericles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pericles-security-threat-model))
- **pericles-skill-authoring**: How to author a Skill manifest and lift an existing Mastra tool or agent into the Skill Registry. ([Pericles-Cloud/pericles](https://github.com/Pericles-Cloud/pericles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pericles-skill-authoring))
- **pericles-skill-registry**: How the Skill Registry resolves, validates, and composes Skills at runtime. Use this WHENEVER you build or change the registry loader, compose a Skill Stack, resolve dependencies, validate pipeline position, pin versions, or wire lineage. Encodes composition-time validation (&lt;100ms/Skill), tenant→platform namespace resolution, the acyclic dependency graph, version pinning, and the link to per-invocation lineage. - **pericles-tech-stack**: The authoritative technology stack for Pericles. Use this WHENEVER you choose a library, queue, datastore, agent framework, or AI provider — and ALWAYS when a PRD, old note, or a .cursor/rules file points you at VoltAgent, Kafka, Redis, RabbitMQ, Celery, or Neon, because those are stale/generic and the doctrine + package.json supersede them. Encodes Mastra + PostgreSQL + OpenAI and the deliberate no-Kafka/no-Redis decision, verified against the live dependencies. ([Pericles-Cloud/pericles](https://github.com/Pericles-Cloud/pericles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pericles-tech-stack))
